\DoxyCodeLine{00034\ \textcolor{comment}{/*\ Templated\ vector\ type\ and\ associated\ interfaces.}}

\DoxyCodeLine{00040\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ There\ are\ both\ 'index'\ and\ 'iterate'\ accessors.\ \ The\ index\ accessor}}

\DoxyCodeLine{00044\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ can\ be\ optimized\ away.}}

\DoxyCodeLine{00049\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ (it\ dies\ if\ there\ is\ not).\ \ The\ latter\ will\ reallocate\ the}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00050\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ vector,\ if\ needed.\ \ Reallocation\ causes\ an\ exponential\ increase\ in}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00051\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ vector\ size.\ \ If\ you\ know\ you\ will\ be\ adding\ N\ elements,\ it\ would}}

\DoxyCodeLine{00054\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ least\ as\ many\ elements\ as\ you\ ask\ for,\ it\ will\ exponentially}}

\DoxyCodeLine{00059\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ specific\ size\ from\ the\ get\ go.}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00060\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00061\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ You\ should\ prefer\ the\ push\ and\ pop\ operations,\ as\ they\ append\ and}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00062\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ remove\ from\ the\ end\ of\ the\ vector.\ If\ you\ need\ to\ remove\ several}}

\DoxyCodeLine{00071\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00072\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ Vectors\ are\ template\ types\ with\ three\ arguments:\ the\ type\ of\ the}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00073\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ elements\ in\ the\ vector,\ the\ allocation\ strategy,\ and\ the\ physical}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00074\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ layout\ to\ use}}
\DoxyCodeLine{00075\ \textcolor{comment}{}}

\DoxyCodeLine{00077\ \textcolor{comment}{}}

\DoxyCodeLine{00080\ \textcolor{comment}{}}

\DoxyCodeLine{00088\ \textcolor{comment}{\ \ \ \ \ \ GC\ activities.}}

\DoxyCodeLine{01509\ \textcolor{comment}{}}
